## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的 AI 搭档。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal AI partner. For example, I can fix a failing test in one of your repos: tell me which repo and I'll get it started.
```

### greet-ko

```

  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 이 컴퓨터에서 바로 할 수 있는 일을 맡길 수 있어요. 예를 들면 특정 저장소에서 실패하는 테스트를 고쳐 달라고 하거나, 메일·일정을 확인해 달라고 하거나, 웹에서 가격이나 예약 정보를 찾아 달라고 하면 돼요. 오늘 무엇부터 해 볼까요?
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCP は「届いたことを保証する・順番どおりに届ける」、UDP は「速さ優先で、保証はしない」です。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para actualizar tu rama local con los cambios de la rama principal antes de compartirla, cuando quieras un historial lineal. Usa merge para integrar una rama que ya está publicada o que otras personas usan, porque reescribir commits compartidos causa problemas.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、面向连接的传输，保证数据按序、不丢不重地送达；UDP 则是不可靠、无连接的，只管尽快发出去，不保证到达，换来的是低延迟和低开销。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own unshared feature branch to tidy its commits onto the latest base before merging. Merge when the branch is shared or public, since rebasing rewrites history that others may already have.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 直接把文件路径传给 `bun test` 就行：

```bash
bun test test/math.test.ts
```

如果只想跑某个用例，加 `-t`（或 `--test-name-pattern`），后面跟用例名的一部分：

```bash
bun test test/math.test.ts -t "加法"
```

另外，`bun test` 后面跟的不一定要完整路径，给一个关键词也行，它会匹配文件名里包含这个词的测试文件。
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log records every change to a database on disk before the change is applied to the main data files. After a crash, the database replays that log to restore committed changes and discard incomplete ones.
```

<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">周五北京到上海 · 600 元以下</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="count">—</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">每天早上更新</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">班次与票价</h2>
<div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
<div class="k-empty" id="empty" hidden>还没有数据，明早会更新。</div>
</main>
<style>
.k-list .k-row-value.k-low { color: var(--d-good); }
</style>
<script>
function esc(s) { return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) {
  return { "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", "\"": "&quot;", "'": "&#39;" }[c];
}); }
function render() {
  var fares = ducter.get('fares') || [];
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  var empty = document.getElementById('empty');
  var count = document.getElementById('count');
  var sub = document.getElementById('sub');
  if (!fares.length) {
    list.innerHTML = '';
    empty.hidden = false;
    count.innerHTML = '<span class="k-none">还没看</span>';
    return;
  }
  empty.hidden = true;
  var under = fares.filter(function (f) { return f.price < 600; });
  count.textContent = under.length + ' 班';
  sub.textContent = under.length ? '600 元以下的班次见下' : '暂时没有 600 元以下的班次';
  list.innerHTML = fares.map(function (f) {
    var cls = f.price < 600 ? 'k-low' : '';
    return '<div class="k-row">' +
      '<div class="k-row-main">' + esc(f.no) + '</div>' +
      '<div class="k-row-sub">' + esc(f.left || '') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="k-row-value ' + cls + '">¥' + esc(f.price) + '</div>' +
      '</div>';
  }).join('');
  ducter.summary(under.length + ' 班在 600 元以下');
}
ducter.onChange(render);
render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"fares":[{"no":"G1","price":553,"left":"二等座余 12 张"},{"no":"G3","price":553,"left":"二等座余 4 张"},{"no":"G7","price":626,"left":"二等座"}]}

function toggle(h, d) {
  const arr = data[h] || (data[h] = []);
  const i = arr.indexOf(d);
  if (i >= 0) arr.splice(i, 1); else arr.push(d);
  ducter.set('ticks', data);
  render();
}
$('#list').addEventListener('click', e => {
  const el = e.target.closest('[data-h][data-d]');
  if (el) toggle(el.dataset.h, el.dataset.d);
});
Promise.resolve(ducter.get('ticks')).then(v => {
  if (v) data = Object.assign({ reading: [], run: [], phone: [] }, v);
  render();
});
ducter.onChange(() => {
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('ticks')).then(v => {
    if (v) data = Object.assign({ reading: [], run: [], phone: [] }, v);
    render();
  });
});

  const ul=document.getElementById("list");
  ul.innerHTML="";
  if(!xs.length){const e=document.createElement("li");e.className="k-empty";e.textContent="还没有花销";ul.appendChild(e);return;}
  for(const x of xs){
    const li=document.createElement("li");li.className="k-row";
    const main=document.createElement("div");
    const m=document.createElement("div");m.className="k-row-main";m.textContent=x.what;
    const s=document.createElement("div");s.className="k-row-sub";s.textContent=x.day;
    main.appendChild(m);main.appendChild(s);
    const v=document.createElement("div");v.className="k-row-value";v.textContent=fmt(Number(x.yuan)||0);
    li.appendChild(main);li.appendChild(v);ul.appendChild(li);
  }
}
ducter.onChange(draw);draw();
ducter.summary("合计 "+fmt(((ducter.get("expenses")||[]).reduce((s,x)=>s+(Number(x.yuan)||0),0))));

    });

  function load() {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get(KEY)).then(function (v) {
      items = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
      render();
    });
  }

  renderCats();
  load();
  ducter.onChange(load);

  document.getElementById('list').addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var del = e.target.closest('[data-del]');
    if (del) {
      var id = del.getAttribute('data-del');
      feeds = feeds.filter(function (f) { return f.id !== id; });
      save();
      render();
      return;
    }
    var row = e.target.closest('.k-row');
    if (row) row.classList.toggle('open');
  });
})();

### app-prs-known-en

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Done: the page is on your shelf with the four PRs, and tapping each one ticks it off as landed.
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1924 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">Waiting on someone</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="count">4</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">PRs still open</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">Tap to tick off when it lands</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
<script>
var ITEMS = ducter.get("items") || [];
var items = ITEMS;

function paint() {
  var open = items.filter(function (i) { return !i.done; }).length;
  var landed = items.length - open;
  document.getElementById('count').textContent = open;
  document.getElementById('sub').textContent = open + ' open, ' + landed + ' landed';
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  list.innerHTML = '';
  items.forEach(function (i) {
    var row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'k-row';
    var box = document.createElement('button');
    box.className = 'k-chip';
    box.setAttribute('aria-pressed', i.done ? 'true' : 'false');
    box.textContent = i.done ? '☑' : '☐';
    box.onclick = function () { i.done = !i.done; save(); paint(); };
    var main = document.createElement('div');
    main.className = 'k-row-main';
    main.innerHTML = '<div></div><div class="k-row-sub"></div>';
    main.firstChild.textContent = i.t;
    main.lastChild.textContent = i.done ? 'Landed' : i.s;
    if (i.done) main.style.textDecoration = 'line-through';
    row.appendChild(box);
    row.appendChild(main);
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
  ducter.summary(open + ' waiting, ' + landed + ' landed');
}

function save() { ducter.set('prs', items); }

function load(v) {
  if (Array.isArray(v) && v.length) items = v;
  paint();
}

var got = ducter.get('prs');
if (got && typeof got.then === 'function') { got.then(load); } else { load(got); }
ducter.onChange(function () {
  var g = ducter.get('prs');
  if (g && typeof g.then === 'function') { g.then(load); } else { load(g); }
});
</script>
</main>; its data now (what it opens with): {"prs":[{"id":"d412","t":"wey-gu/ducter#412 · compaction cut","s":"Waiting on Weizhen’s review since Mon","done":false},{"id":"m10287","t":"nowledge-co/mem#10287 · retry on 502","s":"On CI, failing","done":false},{"id":"h1592","t":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 · flaky compaction test","s":"Waiting on Alice since Tue","done":false},{"id":"d418","t":"wey-gu/ducter#418 · apps bar","s":"Draft","done":false}],"items":[{"id":"d412","t":"wey-gu/ducter#412 · compaction cut","s":"Waiting on Weizhen’s review since Mon","done":false},{"id":"m10287","t":"nowledge-co/mem#10287 · retry on 502","s":"On CI, failing","done":false},{"id":"h1592","t":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 · flaky compaction test","s":"Waiting on Alice since Tue","done":false},{"id":"d418","t":"wey-gu/ducter#418 · apps bar","s":"Draft","done":false}]}
